HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001635/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_03386ee0036b07e18724","text":"1. **The denial is demonstrated as continuous administration.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `و د ع:B001` is leaving or cutting off, fixed `ق ل ي:B003` is heart-expelling aversion, and fixed `ر ب ب:B002` is stage-by-stage nurture and completion. **Structural cue:** 93:6–8 repeat `وَوَجَدَكَ/يَجِدْكَ` and answer three conditions with the immediate-result pattern `فَـَٔاوَىٰ / فَهَدَىٰ / فَأَغْنَىٰ`. **Assigned role:** nurture is the continuing process; the three reversals are its observable traces. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies the repeated grammar and outcomes; I infer outcomes → evidence of an administering relation that had not ceased. **Reading change:** the ayah becomes more than affective reassurance: it states a continuity that the following case-history tests and exhibits.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000070/B001","root_001253/B002","root_001635/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_04457833b217695a02e5","text":"2. **Neither final farewell nor expulsion fits the trajectory.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `و د ع:B003` is farewell at departure; fixed `ق ل ي:B002` is driving or casting away; 93:6 supplies shelter (`ء و ي:B001`), and 93:7–8 supply guidance and sufficiency. **Structural cue:** both separation-images are negated before a sequence that gathers, directs, and equips. **Assigned role:** farewell supplies temporal finality and casting-away supplies spatial exclusion; the later verbs reverse both. **Abductive move:** the packet gives these opposed motions; I infer shelter/direction/sufficiency → continued inclusion. **Reading change:** the double negation blocks two distinct fears at once: that the relation ended and that the addressee was pushed outside its care.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_07a5e96f2d3190421c3a","text":"The later triad 93:6-8 made \"your Lord has not left you\" much more concrete than the first pass: ربّ became a history of finding, sheltering, guiding, and sufficing. 93:9-10 then surprised me by exporting the negation outward: if the Lord did not abandon or hate, the addressee must not enact abandonment through قهر or نهر toward others.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000679/B001","root_001635/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_08f263ae4aef3ed776fb","text":"4. **Restful non-interference rather than neglect.** Evidence: و د ع B002 (restful ease and calm; obtaining without strain), س ج و B001 in 93:2 (still settling), ر ض و B001 in 93:5 (acceptance), ع ط و B006 in 93:5 (yielding/compliant ease). Structural cue: night-stillness and future satisfaction surround the negation. Mechanism: the packet supplies rest/ease branches; I supply the alternative that being \"left\" in quiet can mean allowed repose rather than abandoned. Abductive move: the same surface interval can be read by outcome: if it ends in giving and satisfaction, it was repose. Reading change: the denial becomes subtle: your Lord has not left you in the bad sense, and even the apparent leaving may be a gentle easing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001635/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_105fddc7eb02d2e8be9e","text":"2. **Farewell/death scenario negated.** Evidence: و د ع B003 (farewell for traveler/living/dead with peace), س ج و B002 in 93:2 (shrouding), ء خ ر B003 in 93:4 (rear/posterior), ق ه ر B005 in 93:9 (backward retreat). Structural cue: covered night precedes a verb whose branch includes leave-taking of the living or dead; later \"last/rear\" and retreat branches intensify directionality away. Mechanism: the packet supplies shroud/farewell/rear imagery; I supply the scenario that the addressee could read silence as being sent away or consigned behind. Abductive move: 93:3 denies a whole departure-image, not just a lexical abandonment. Reading change: \"has not bidden you farewell\" becomes a negation of deathlike separation and backward removal.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001253/B002","root_001635/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1909205e8c1df131c4b6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:3:2\",\"root_001635\",[\"B003\"]],[\"93:3:5\",\"root_001253\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İki olumsuzlama iki ayrı korkuyu kapatır: ilişkinin son bir vedayla bitmesini ve kişinin bakım alanının dışına itilmesini.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001253/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4d196b7f1eb8502e3fd1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:3:5\",\"root_001253\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Darılmanın kalpten dışarı atma boyutu, açılıştaki merhamet ve sonraki şefkatli barındırmayla tersine çevrilir; reddedilen yalnız dış ihmal değil, içten dışlamadır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_001635/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_62d5a0da1b0779def8a8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:3:2\",\"root_001635\",[\"B001\"]],[\"93:3:3\",\"root_000532\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Terk etmeme, sonraki barındırma, yol gösterme ve yeterli kılma örnekleriyle, Rabbin aşama aşama süren bakımının somut bir sürekliliği olarak açıklığa kavuşur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001253/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6c51043258675a4d167a","text":"3. **No casting out of the heart.** Evidence: ق ل ي B003 (aversion that casts something out of the heart), ق ل ي B002 (drive/cast away), ر ح م B001/B002 in 93:0 (tenderness; kinship bond), ء و ي B002 in 93:6 (compassionate turning). Structural cue: negated hatred is anchored by opening mercy and later sheltering pity. Mechanism: the packet supplies aversion/casting away and mercy/pity; I supply the affective inversion that divine relation is centripetal, not expulsive. Abductive move: hatred would expel from the heart; mercy gathers back. Reading change: the ayah denies internal rejection, not merely outward neglect.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000679/B001","root_001635/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9d4efb84d63d9309404e","text":"3. **Stillness can be respite without being desertion.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `و د ع:B002` is restful ease and settled calm; 93:2 supplies still settling through `س ج و:B001`, and 93:5 supplies giving that leads to `ر ض و:B001`, acceptance rather than displeasure. **Structural cue:** calm precedes the denial, while satisfaction follows it. **Assigned role:** the ease branch distinguishes a quiet interval from the abandonment branch of the same root. **Abductive move:** I infer that a pause can serve care when the wider sequence continues toward provision; the packet does not state the purpose of the pause. **Reading change:** the ayah allows apparent inactivity to be read as rest within an active relation, not as its cancellation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001635/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ae0b8e9afad5b92b06a8","text":"1. **No abandonment because Lordship is ongoing nurture.** Evidence: و د ع B001 (leaving/letting go), ر ب ب B002 (repair, nurture, completion), ر ب ب B007 (staying, lasting), ء و ي B001 in 93:6 (sheltering/gathering). Structural cue: negated leaving is followed by three recollections of finding and remedial action. Mechanism: the packet supplies negation of leaving and repeated later care acts; I supply the inference that ربّ means continuing management through stages. Abductive move: if the Lord repairs stage by stage, a silent interval cannot be abandonment. Reading change: the ayah denies not only emotional rejection but the failure of a caretaker's process.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001253/B002","root_001635/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_beb408676e6d614ec746","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:3:2\",\"root_001635\",[\"B001\"]],[\"93:3:5\",\"root_001253\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Terk edilmemiş ve dışlanmamış olma deneyimi, sonraki yetimi ezmeme ve isteyeni azarlamama buyruklarıyla, başkalarını dışlamama sorumluluğuna dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001635/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f3093d194f74565a8b31","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:3:2\",\"root_001635\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Terk etme kökündeki dinlenmeye bırakma boyutu, görünürdeki hareketsizliği ihmalden ayırır ve onu süren bakım içindeki bir soluklanma olarak okumaya imkan verir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001253/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_faa7944ba0412ca01b9f","text":"93:6–8 supplied the retrospective proof: their repeated finding-and-reversal grammar changed the double denial from an unsupported assurance into a claim evidenced by shelter, direction, and sufficiency.
